Final architecture plan · current disk · 2026-08-20

C# Fault Architecture Rebuild

Replace the mirrored smart-enum estate and nine competing legacy shapes with one package-local, typed fault model: Thinktecture closes each family, one explicit ordinal generates its numeric identity, LanguageExt carries and accumulates failures, and the kernel owns only reusable mechanics plus the global allocation ledger.

sole artifactplanning corpus: read-only censusno package admissionno new implementation filesno compatibility paradigm

01 · Architectural ruling

One model: a fault is a local closed Thinktecture union case with typed payload, an explicit append-only offset, a kernel band, and generated identity. It is already a LanguageExt Error; Fin, Validation<Error,T>, Eff/IO, and Error.Many are the only rails.
KEEP
FaultBand as the single branch-wide ABI allocation ledger. It allocates ranges and owners; it never enumerates downstream cases.
REBUILD
The project base currently named Expected becomes Fault, directly deriving LanguageExt.Common.Error. The current kernel union named Fault becomes KernelFault.
DELETE
FaultCategory, all 113 mirrored XCategory : IFaultRegistry<XCategory> types, IFaultRegistry, FaultRegistry, RegistryFault<>, family aggregate carriers, and handwritten code/category switches.
GENERATE
The existing analyzer/generator assembly emits one cached numeric identity per sealed leaf from [FaultCase(n)]; it diagnoses missing, duplicated, negative, open/unclosed branches, and handwritten competing members.

Non-negotiable invariants

  1. Every project-defined expected failure derives from the one Fault base. Unknown thrown failures remain LanguageExt exceptional Error values; they are not re-minted from Message.
  2. Every bounded package recovery vocabulary is one local [Union] root. Kernel contains mechanics and universal substrate failures only—not a monorepo case catalog.
  3. One FaultBand row identifies one semantic fault family, and one in-band offset identifies one direct sealed record leaf. Thinktecture record unions do not support abstract intermediate record cases; shared cause and policy stay in typed leaf payload or root functions. Numeric history never forces unrelated domains to merge; a second genuine family receives a distinct owner-qualified band.
  4. Category, generic Case, package, band, and offset are removed from public identity. FaultId equality exposes the globally allocated numeric Code alone; band/offset are generator inputs and owner is a local C# observation derived by FaultBand.OwnerOf(BandKind.Fault, code). The in-process union type remains the typed semantic discriminator.
  5. Payload and recovery remain authored on the leaf; uniform message/detail behavior may sit once on the family root, as the exemplar does. Posture is immutable or derived from immutable typed payload/root data, never caller-settable. Stable identity is generated. Presentation, logging, wire, and notices are projections and cannot recreate taxonomy.
  6. Offsets are explicit constants. Declaration order, hashes, runtime type names, package-internal metadata, and string parsing are forbidden identity sources. Reflection is test-only built-estate discovery, never runtime identity.
  7. No adapter phase lands. Tooling may be proved on isolated specimens, but the product estate converts atomically and the branch never carries old and new public paradigms together.
  8. No implementation helper files are added. Existing owner pages, the existing generator, existing test-kit files, and existing wire/projection pages absorb the work.
Rhino and Grasshopper are full replacements, not compatibility lanes. The seven Rhino direct-Expected roots and GhFault end as ordinary canonical Fault unions. Their old constructors, offset/message switches, hard-coded absolute codes, drift guards, local aggregates, PinKeyError, and message-remint adapters are removed in the same slices. RhinoCommon/GH/CLR exceptions exist only as foreign boundary inputs or unavoidable host-ABI egress after typed evidence is persisted; they never form a competing expected-failure shape.
Numerics is in scope now. GeometryFault is rebuilt directly on this model with all 27 typed payload cases; FaultCluster, Seat, parser, GeometryError, and ToError delete in the atomic rewrite. §08 pins every old and final geometry code.

The census extracted only csharp fences from libs/csharp/**/*.md, excluding .api, bin, generated output, and vendor source. Structural ownership—not filename suffix—determined a fault family. Planning pages were never compiled, tested, or passed to code analyzers.

Structural decomposition

SurfaceFamilies / casesAuthored LOCFinding

Construction counts are ripple size, not deletion credit. The complete, comment-stripped invocation predicate finds 4,171 constructions; the earlier 4,065 suffix-only count is withdrawn. The campaign may shorten construction shape but cannot claim domain observations as duplicate LOC.

03 · Correctness defects that precede refactoring

PriorityVerified defectRequired resolution
P0IdentityCategory.ModelIncomplete uses offset 10 in StoreIdentity span 10 and collides at code 8350 with Columnar.Query.Final identity is offset 0 / code 8340; existing offsets 1–9 stay fixed. Persisted 8350 rows are rewritten only when owner/context proves the source; ambiguous rows are quarantined, never guessed.
P0CoordinationCategory.Unmapped uses offset 10 in StoreCoordination span 10; raw code 8440 crosses into the GeoIngest interval.Final identity is offset 0 / code 8430; existing offsets 1–9 stay fixed. No band widening or reader alias is introduced.
P0FaultBand.Disjoint is a Lazy with no executable read; the claimed global type-initialization proof never runs.Force it inside every FaultId construction, before code issuance, and independently prove the built estate in the existing architecture-test surface.
P0Almost every kernel fault inherits LanguageExt code 0; only Unsupported pins 9104. Telemetry therefore emits owner Unclassified.Give every retained KernelFault case an explicit offset — 14 after the generic Text case deletes under the §07 closure. Preserve Unsupported = 9104.
P0Eight legacy unions call base(Message: string.Empty, Code: 0), but the current project Expected exposes only a parameterless constructor. PluginFault also uses generated Switch without [Union].Delete the stale constructor pattern through canonical migration; do not repair the dead shape.
P0One AppHost path throws CommandFault.ExecutionFaulted even though a LanguageExt Error is not an Exception.Return/park the typed fault, then throw only the host ABI exception after the required receipt is persisted.
P0Validation<TFault,T> appears 363 times across 19 names. The generic type itself is unconstrained, so the shape spells fine in a design fence, but every construction and applicative entry point requires F : Monoid<F> (CS0311) — forcing each family to hand-roll Combine plus an Empty fault value. That empty fault is the unlawful identity the aggregate carrier cases exist to supply.Convert the complete estate to Validation<Error,T> and delete family aggregate carriers. Error already implements Monoid<Error> and Semigroup<Error>, so no family algebra survives the conversion.
P1Op.Catch decides cancellation by Error.HasException<OperationCanceledException>(), which recurses into derived cancellations and admits any library's internal cancel as the caller's.Take the execution token as a parameter and mint the cancelled case only when that token proves requested; delete the class test and its "derived cancellations included" law at the owning kernel rails page.
P1Op.Catch turns every unknown non-cancellation exception into InvalidResult(error.Message), discarding exception type, stack, inner error, and provider evidence.Preserve unknown exceptional Error; classify only documented provider failures through an owner-supplied mapper after cancellation.
P1TimeFault borrows FaultBand.Config offsets 10–12. Per-roster soundness cannot prove cross-family uniqueness.Create the already-owed owner-qualified HostSchedule band, migrate 4110–4112 explicitly, and enforce one band ↔ one family.
P1Theme/Typography and Cache/WideColumn split one identity range across two roots.One band ↔ one semantic family is an invariant, never a numeric merge heuristic. Theme is one bounded context, so Theme absorbs Typography. Generic Cache absorbs provider-specific WideColumn; the full old 8450/10 interval is replaced by Cache = 5500/span 50, cases use 5500–5509, and the provider-named root/band delete.
P135 raw Error.New sites, 40 redundant FabricationFault.ToError() calls, and generic string buckets erase typed cause at high-volume seams.Classify each site at source; preserve foreign Error, mint typed owner faults only for expected refusals, and remove redundant conversions.
Numeric correction is a separate gate from code generation. A generator must not make the two existing invalid rows look repaired by moving annotations. The old code, the authorized replacement, persisted/wire consumers, and the migration decision must be visible before bulk conversion.

04 · Target mechanism

Package casetyped payload, Message, dynamic Retriability

Kernel contract

The base carries no message field, category string, provider dictionary, metadata bag, or downstream type registry. This avoids LanguageExt Expected's backing-state equality/copy trap while retaining its expected-error protocol.

using LanguageExt.Common;

namespace Rasm.Domain;

[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Class, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = false)]
public sealed class FaultCaseAttribute(int offset) : Attribute
{
    public int Offset { get; } = offset;
}

public sealed record FaultId
{
    internal FaultId(FaultBand band, int offset)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(band);
        _ = FaultBand.Disjoint;
        if (band.Kind is not BandKind.Fault)
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(band), band, "Fault identity requires a fault band.");
        }

        Code = band.Code(offset);
    }

    public int Code { get; }
}

public interface ICausedFault
{
    Error Cause { get; }
}

public abstract record Fault : Error
{
    protected abstract FaultId IdentityCore { get; }
    protected static FaultId Identify(FaultBand band, int offset) => new(band, offset);

    public FaultId Identity => IdentityCore;
    public sealed override int Code => Identity.Code;
    public sealed override bool IsExpected => true;
    public sealed override bool IsExceptional => false;
    public sealed override Option<Error> Inner =>
        this is ICausedFault caused ? Some(caused.Cause) : None;
    public sealed override ErrorException ToErrorException() =>
        new WrappedErrorExpectedException(this);

    public virtual Retriability Retriability => Retriability.Terminal;
}

Authored family

[FaultCase] is one public mechanical attribute beside Fault in the existing kernel owner page. Emitting a private copy into every compilation would multiply metadata types and weaken built-estate reflection. A redundant family marker does not exist: [Union] plus direct Fault inheritance identifies the root. No second analyzer project or helper file is introduced. The 37 AppUi aliases, 24 divergent keys, and *Case suffixes are removed as code cleanup, but no replacement runtime string token is minted.

using LanguageExt.Common;
using Thinktecture;

namespace Rasm.AppHost.Runtime;

[Union(ConversionFromValue = ConversionOperatorsGeneration.None)]
public abstract partial record LaneFault : Fault
{
    private LaneFault(string detail) => Detail = detail;
    private static readonly FaultBand FamilyBand = FaultBand.LaneGuard;

    public string Detail { get; }
    public sealed override string Message => Detail;

    [FaultCase(1)]
    public sealed partial record CallerLeft(Op Operation)
        : LaneFault($"lane caller left: {Operation.Key}");

    [FaultCase(2)]
    public sealed partial record Rejected(Op Operation, LaneReason Reason)
        : LaneFault($"lane rejected: {Operation.Key}:{Reason.Key}");

    [FaultCase(3)]
    public sealed partial record Shed(Op Operation, int QueueDepth)
        : LaneFault($"lane shed: {Operation.Key}:depth={QueueDepth}")
    {
        public override Retriability Retriability => Retriability.Transient;
    }
}

Generated leaf surface

The generator caches one validated FaultId and overrides one protected member per case. It does not generate Message, payload, retry policy, aggregation, exception mapping, domain factories, or string identity. When all leaves share detail rendering, the family root keeps one real detail field/Message override and its leaves pass their typed rendering to that constructor; case-specific rendering stays on the leaf. This lowest-common-owner rule prevents the generator migration from multiplying three-line Message bodies.

namespace Rasm.AppHost.Runtime;

public abstract partial record LaneFault
{
    public sealed partial record CallerLeft
    {
        private static readonly FaultId FaultIdentity = Identify(FamilyBand, 1);
        protected sealed override FaultId IdentityCore => FaultIdentity;
    }

    public sealed partial record Rejected
    {
        private static readonly FaultId FaultIdentity = Identify(FamilyBand, 2);
        protected sealed override FaultId IdentityCore => FaultIdentity;
    }

    public sealed partial record Shed
    {
        private static readonly FaultId FaultIdentity = Identify(FamilyBand, 3);
        protected sealed override FaultId IdentityCore => FaultIdentity;
    }
}

Generator proof boundary

Compile-time, inside one consumer compilationRuntime / built-estate proofHuman semantic review
Top-level non-generic abstract partial record with [Union], private constructor, Fault base, and exactly one root-owned private static readonly FaultBand FamilyBand = FaultBand.<row> binding. Every case is a direct, non-generic, sealed partial record carrying exactly one constant nonnegative ordinal. The generator proves exclusive band ownership, fully qualified hint names, and no competing identity/code members.FaultBand.Code proves span containment; the FaultId constructor forces FaultBand.Disjoint before issuing any identity. The existing architecture spec reads each root's FamilyBand, each leaf's public FaultCaseAttribute.Offset, and generated static FaultIdentity.Code after RuntimeHelpers.RunClassConstructor; it derives offset/code consistency and proves cross-assembly family/band exclusivity and global uniqueness without manufacturing invalid records.Correct owner, evidence shape, message, retriability, cause policy, redaction, old-code migration, and whether a case is a genuine fault rather than absence/evidence/invariant. Shared leaf repetition may be extracted only into an already-real payload vocabulary or root function, never an abstract record branch.

Roslyn cannot evaluate the base/span constructor arguments of a referenced static readonly FaultBand. Duplicating base/span into attributes, constants, an AdditionalFile, or a generated manifest would create a second authority and is forbidden.

05 · Rails, validation, capture, and recovery

One rail vocabulary

ConcernCanonical shapeDeleted shape
Public fallible operationFin<T>Custom Result, bool + parked text, thrown expected fault
Independent admissionValidation<Error,T>; lower once with ToFin()Validation<PackageFault,T>, family Semigroup/Aggregate/Combine
Dependent admissionFin<T>/BindApplicative accumulation of checks whose later inputs do not exist after an earlier failure
Effect executionIO/Eff over an explicit try/catch inside the shared boundary captureRepeated Try.lift().Run().MapFail().Bind(identity) funnels, which normalize before any rule runs
AccumulationError.Many, Error + Error, AsIterable()18 local Aggregate cases and six-line flattening switches
Ordinary absenceOption<T> until a consumer owes a reason“not found” faults at no-obligation probes
Evidence alongside successExisting receipt/Writer shapeTurning measurements, warnings, or benchmark rows into terminal faults

Thinktecture validation

Generated value-object admission uses Thinktecture’s default ValidationError as ephemeral factory evidence only. It cannot cross an owner boundary, receive a code/recovery policy, or become the accumulated public error type. The kernel acceptance bridge immediately turns it into KernelFault.InvalidValue and enters Validation<Error,T>. Scalar predicates continue to use the one typed KernelFault.OutOfRange. Package unions no longer implement IValidationError<TPackageFault> merely to satisfy a generator.

  • A universal value invariant—blank, range, finite, positive, malformed representation—belongs to the kernel bridge.
  • An irreducible package semantic rule is a typed gate over the raw request before construction; only its success enters generated normalization/representation admission. It returns its package fault explicitly and is never hidden in Create(string).
  • The 449 Fabrication custom-error attributes, 42 AppUi family factories, 21 unused Persistence factories, and nine mismatched callback signatures are classified under this rule. No sentinel payload such as <none>, SqlState = none, or an arbitrary policy value survives.

Boundary capture hook

Op.Catch remains the only reusable capture mechanism. Every token-aware overload receives the exact execution token handed to the body. It maps a thrown OperationCanceledException to KernelFault.Cancelled only when that token is requested; an unrequested OCE and every tokenless-overload OCE pass through exact capture and the provider classifier. Capture is an explicit try/catch over the body: Try.lift(...).Run() normalizes a thrown cancellation or timeout into an Expected error at a reserved negative code with the exception dropped and splays an AggregateException into ManyErrors — flipping its inner timeout too — so every rule below would run against evidence the lift already destroyed, and IO.lift rethrows cancellation rather than capturing it. Every caught exception is captured with Error.New(raised.Message, raised) through a variable whose static type is Exception: a derived static type makes that call ambiguous between New(string, Exception) and New(string, Error) (CS0121, via the implicit Exception-to-Error conversion), so the cancellation arm widens or casts before capture. Error.New(raised) is forbidden outright: on the installed build it does not merely lose evidence, it changes the classification. A cancellation or timeout exception becomes an Expected error at a reserved negative code with IsExceptional false and the exception dropped — indistinguishable from Errors.Cancelled — and an AggregateException is splayed into ManyErrors children instead of remaining one captured failure. A returned failed Fin<T> passes through unchanged. The provider classifier is generic Func<Error,Option<TFault>> where TFault : Fault, ICausedFault, making original-cause custody a type constraint; None returns the exact captured error. Sync, async, and body-returning-Fin overloads flatten exactly once. CatchM is permitted only after capture for predicate-selected recovery; it never performs exception admission. Run-all batches use LanguageExt PartitionFallible so successes and failures both survive.

Input failureResultRule
Thrown OperationCanceledException with requested execution tokenKernelFault.Cancelled with the exact caught exception as Cause/InnerCatch before LanguageExt normalization; only the requested token proves caller cancellation.
Unrequested/tokenless OCE, TimeoutException, AggregateExceptionExact Exceptional from Error.New(raised.Message, raised), then provider classifierPreserve the outer exception reference, type, stack, and message; never infer cancellation from exception class alone.
Pre-existing Errors.CancelledForeign terminal ErrorIts discarded exception cannot be reconstructed or fabricated.
Documented provider exception/statusOwner case with structured provider fields and InnerClassifier lives beside the package boundary; no kernel provider taxonomy.
Unknown exceptionOriginal LanguageExt exceptional ErrorPreserve type, stack, message, and cause; no terminal expected remint.
Documented false/null/Try missTyped expected owner case, minted at the call siteThese are return-contract refusals, not exceptions.
Programmer/static invariantCLR exceptionThrowIfNull, disposed-object guards, and impossible composition states remain exceptional.
No provider taxonomy or classifier service is introduced. ICausedFault is the single mechanical cause hook; a static, partial owner function returning Option<TCausedFault> keeps provider dependencies out of kernel, composes with sync/async capture, and makes the mapped arm retain the original error by constraint.

Recovery algebra

Retriability remains the sole recovery discriminant. One kernel Redrive.Posture(Error) recursively folds the ManyErrors membership tree—not Inner, which is causal evidence. AsIterable() and Count are both direct-child views — a nested aggregate stays one opaque child — so the fold recurses until non-ManyErrors leaves and never tests emptiness by count. A scalar Error yields itself from AsIterable(), so one uniform recursion covers scalar and aggregate with no type test, and Errors.None is a zero-child aggregate rather than a leaf. Adding context or cleanup failures therefore cannot accidentally hide recovery behind an aggregate wrapper.

Leaf setFolded postureSettlement
Any non-Fault scalar Error, exceptional or expected, or any Terminal Fault leafTerminalDo not retry; foreign identities never become retryable by accident.
No Terminal; one or more Throttled leavesThrottled with the greatest required RetryAfterDefer to the governing window; never shorten provider advice. A negative duration is invalid at construction/admission.
Only Transient leavesTransientApply the bounded schedule.
Empty / Errors.NoneInvalid terminal constructionNever retry.

Error.Is for project faults compares numeric identity only; record Equals compares case plus payload. Neither method routes recovery. Retriability is the only recovery authority, including after recursive ManyErrors folding.

Redrive is the sole in-process re-drive executor. Polly exists only at the outbound HTTP hop and owns that hop's timeout, circuit breaker, limiter, chaos, and retry; an HTTP operation is never wrapped by Redrive. Every non-HTTP deadline/retry remains LanguageExt IO + Redrive, while admission uses the actual lane/BCL rate-limiter owner. One HTTP provider decision table drives both Polly's raw Outcome<HttpResponseMessage> predicate and the final typed fault/retriability, preventing two authored classifications.

Generic-string closure

Final target: zero generic text faults. No fault leaf is named Text/TextCase, and no catch-all leaf has only string Detail as semantic payload. A string may remain presentation evidence only after a typed case or closed typed payload already carries identity, reason, context, cause, and recovery.
Current use bucketFamiliesFinal disposition

AppUi: Chart, Session, Tour, Proof, Board, Content, Notebook, Search, Draft, Viewport, Layout.Each producer maps to an existing semantic leaf, a universal KernelFault, preserved foreign Error, or a named closed typed reason payload. The old Text leaf and offset delete.

No reservation or replacement type is created.

The source decision order is closed: generated validation → kernel bridge; Error/Exception Message remint → original Error unless an explicit cause-bearing provider mapper exists; probe miss → Option until obligation, then an existing typed missing/input case; documented false/null refusal → typed owner case with call evidence; observation → receipt/telemetry; impossible state → CLR exception; remaining domain refusal → a named typed case or closed payload arm already listed by its owner. There is no generic fallback, and G2 is blocked by any producer without a final typed constructor.

Final generic wire schema and admission

message FaultRecovery {
  oneof kind {
    google.protobuf.Empty terminal = 1;
    google.protobuf.Empty transient = 2;
    google.protobuf.Duration retry_after = 3;
  }
}

message FaultDetail {
  reserved 1, 3, 5;
  reserved "package", "case", "evidence";
  int32 code = 2;
  string message = 4;
  string correlation = 6;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp hlc_physical = 7;
  uint64 hlc_logical = 8;
  string tenant = 9;
  FaultRecovery recovery = 10;
}

Admission rejects an unset recovery arm, negative or malformed retry duration, nonpositive code, invalid timestamp/correlation, or more than one recognized FaultDetail. Unknown Any details coexist. Zero recognized details follow the existing transport classifier; exactly one valid detail becomes WireFault.Remote. Unknown codes remain opaque RemoteFault evidence. The current estate has exactly two behavior-bearing slots—TransactionReceipt.conflict=5 and CommandReply.refusal=4—and neither needs source-union rehydration, so the final schema adds zero RPC-owned fault evidence oneofs. Transaction conflict retains the envelope structurally rather than rendering/reparsing it.

The TypeScript boundary has exactly three outcomes: Remote(detail,recovery) retries in place; Transport(connectivity/deadline/ceiling) alone may drive failover; MalformedDetail is terminal. Delete reason/surface/hops. A remote domain code never controls topology, and TypeScript does not mirror FaultBand.

telemetry/log historystructured event exportersimmutable schema epochDo not rewrite historical observations; interpret under their recorded epoch. New emitters use numeric code and locally derived owner/recovery.

Atomic wire cutover. The same source slice updates the proto, the manifest identity law, Compute wire ownership, TypeScript codec ownership, TypeScript invocation, descriptor proofs, fixtures, and decoders. Before removing the old discriminator, inventory persisted/outbox/cache/wire captures; rewrite transactionally where source context is conclusive, quarantine ambiguous code 8350 rows, interpret historical telemetry under its schema epoch, and reject old transient frames at cutover. No dual reader, alias, fallback, or compatibility field lands.

Kernel code assignment

The only externally pinned kernel code found is Unsupported = 9104. Assign the remaining typed cases explicitly without restriding it; the generic Text case and code 9114 delete:

MissingOperation 9100MissingContext 9101InvalidContext 9102InvalidInput 9103Unsupported 9104
InvalidResult 9105Cancelled 9106ComputationFailed 9107MissingGeometry 9108InvalidGeometry 9109
InvalidValue 9110OutOfRange 9111InvalidUnitSystem 9112Caution 91139114 unissued
 
[FaultCase(6)]
public sealed partial record Cancelled(Error Cause) : KernelFault, ICausedFault
{
    public override string Message => "Operation was cancelled.";
}

Op.Catch constructs Cause only with Error.New(raised.Message, raised) after proving the supplied execution token is requested. Fault.Inner projects ICausedFault.Cause once; no leaf reimplements the cause chain.

Old code-0 custody: Text, MissingOperation, MissingContext, InvalidContext, InvalidInput, InvalidResult, Cancelled, ComputationFailed, MissingGeometry, InvalidGeometry, InvalidValue, OutOfRange, InvalidUnitSystem, and Caution all previously emitted zero; Unsupported alone emitted 9104. In-memory typed values convert structurally. A persisted/wire numeric-only zero is non-invertible: retain it under its historical schema epoch or quarantine it. Never infer a case from Message/context, and ship no final zero alias, decoder fallback, or compatibility map.

Generation and Hook are the only unused fault-band rows. Delete both allocations; add no speculative family. HostSchedule is not speculative: an existing AppHost family currently squats on Config and the owner-qualified name avoids shadowing LanguageExt Schedule.

Domain compression without semantic deletion

Bim and string-taxonomy families

The 220 Bim Detail rows encode repeated scope/action/condition strings, and 331 of 334 mints use the same At constructor. Replace the flat roster with small, real domain axes—scope, operation, refusal reason—and typed optional evidence on the surviving case. The execution receipt below must contain the exact old row → axes tuple before Bim edits begin. This is parametric compression, not a generic dictionary and not 220 new union cases. The four unminted rows (EnergyClassMiss, EnergyFaceMiss, EventEnvelopeMalformed, PatternReject) delete as unused reservations; no error observation is removed.

Exact 220-row Bim compression map

DetailLeg becomes the real 13-row BimScope vocabulary; the five current raise delegates become BimReason. The existing Op argument is the operation/site identity, so the 220 row names and grep tokens are not re-created elsewhere. BimFault.Refused(Op,BimScope,BimReason,string detail) at offset0/code2600 is terminal and the detail is presentation evidence only—never parsed or routed. Captured boundary failures instead mint BimFault.BoundaryFailed(BimBoundary, Error Cause) at offset1/code2601 and implement ICausedFault; immutable boundary data owns posture. BimBoundary is exactly HoneybeeJson, DragonflyJson, OpenStudioRaise, OpenStudioInMemoryDecode, HostScratchWrite, OpenStudioPathDecode, or TessellationCompanion. Aggregate deletes to Error.Many.

Mint-site override: EnergyDecode at Energy/derive 782, 800, 808 and Projection/projector 257, 431, 438, 564 remains terminal Refused(Energy, Rejected,...). Projection/projector 255 and 429 preserve Honeybee/Dragonfly parser causes; 514 preserves the OpenStudio/kernel cause; Tessellation/tessellation 212 preserves the companion cause. Compute generic buckets

Do not replace roughly 274 message grammars with 274 cases or one area/string bucket. Where a package refusal remains owed, one ComputeFault.Violation(ComputeArea, ComputeViolation) leaf at offset0/code2200 carries the closed evidence union. ComputeViolation has exactly eight direct arms: Required(ComputeSubject), Shape(ShapeRequirement,ShapeEvidence), NonFinite(ComputeSubject,ScalarEvidence), Range(RangeRequirement,ScalarEvidence), Unsupported(ComputeCapability), Capacity(CapacityRequirement,CapacityEvidence), Cycle(GraphWitness), and Contract(ComputeContract,ContractEvidence). Those payload vocabularies are typed package data; no slug, dictionary, runtime type name, or open Detail participates in routing. Existing behaviorally named ComputeFault cases remain stronger than the violation carrier. Ban direct ComputeFault.Create and Message rewraps. Flatten SymbolicFault so its four leaves are direct. Unify Convergence, SolveTermination, dual booleans, and receipt booleans into one typed bounded-work result; only an obligated caller maps a nonconverged result into a fault.

Exact 337-site Compute generic-bucket map

The 337 rows are the complete producer census. Each final cell selects an existing semantic case, a universal kernel guard, a typed ComputeViolation arm, the bounded-work result, or the original Error. The 22 cause-bearing MapFail sites retain the captured Error. “Typed evidence” means the existing source locals become the named payload record; it never means copying the old slug into a new string. G2 is blocked if any row still lacks one of these final classes.

Element and universal invariants

Delete AdmissionSlots’ re-casing of finite/range checks into ElementFault.ValueRejected. Universal scalar refusal remains KernelFault.OutOfRange; the reusable applicative lift/accumulation operator belongs in kernel. Element retains graph/address/projection semantics.

Geometry

Make GeometryFault derive Fault and assign the exact sparse offsets in the code map above. Preserve all 27 payload cases, issued codes, and real stage/witness payload vocabularies. Delete FaultCluster, Seat, OfMessage, GeometryError, and ToError; the census found no external semantic consumer for the cluster/parser machinery. Context enrichment preserves the original cause rather than prepending a terminal error.

Rhino and Grasshopper final disposition

These hosts use the same model as every package. The table is the complete surviving Rhino-local identity set; existing sparse offsets/codes remain fixed. Universal blank/missing/illegal input goes to KernelFault.InvalidValue, scalar bounds to KernelFault.OutOfRange, validation funnels to the kernel bridge, Measured to a receipt, unknown host raises to their original exceptional Error, and the six Rhino Aggregate leaves to Error.Many.

Grasshopper keeps exactly five local leaves: Absent(GhSubject) offset1/code4851, ContractRefused(GhContract, GhEvidence) offset2/code4852, Conversion offset3/code4853, Registration offset5/code4855, and Overdue offset6/code4856. Text, Host, Aggregate, Hosted.Bound, absolute-code drift guards, PinKeyError, and Message remints delete. Unknown exceptions remain the original Error; documented GH refusals use the typed local evidence; host ABI throws occur only after RunFact/notice custody.

Exception egress classification

Symbol/siteRoleFinal action
Numerics ReferenceElement.ProveAscendingprogrammer/static invariantKeep the CLR throw unchanged; it is not an expected fault or host egress.
bare catch { cleanup; throw; } in Rhino custody scopesexact rethrow after cleanupKeep; no Error mint and no message wrapper.
Grasshopper Panic, SpecPlugin.OnLoaded, Catalogue.Exportedrequired GH host ABI egressPersist RunFact/typed evidence first, then throw error.ToException(); never new InvalidOperationException(error.Message).
Rhino command/plugin override and callback exitsrequired Rhino host ABI egressPark the exact Error/receipt first; return host status or throw the original/specified ABI exception only where the callback contract requires it.
all other lexical throw hitsmust resolve by symbol, not regex suffixClassify as invariant, exact rethrow, or ABI egress in the 472-file row before editing; expected Fault values are never thrown directly.

Consumer sweep boundaries

The migration is not declaration-only. The ledger below covers all 472 current files matching the declared lexical invocation predicate after C# comments are stripped: a project Fault/*Fault/Error/*Error member invocation in a C# fence. It records direct construction, static factories, Fin.Fail, MapFail, Error.New, catch/throw, ToError, type tests, codes, and category reads. The following high-risk groups receive explicit owner review:

  • All seven Rhino family definitions and their consumers across Annotation, Commands, Display, Document, Exchange, HostUi, Objects, Persistence, Plugin, Render, and Viewport.
  • All seven additional ComputeFault partial segments: Analysis/dispatch, Model/sessions, Runtime/ingest, Runtime/scheduling, Symbolic/dimensional, Symbolic/expression, and Tensor/vocabulary.
  • AppHost Runtime/Wire retry, fault-spine, outbox, companion, coordination, and module activation edges.
  • AppUi’s 977 constructions, Loro exception funnel, LiveData async iterator, six raw errors, root ThrownExceptions edge, and the Theme/Immersive exceptions to simple mirroring.
  • Fabrication’s 449 validation-error owners, 262 admission calls, 40 redundant conversions, and 736 PolicyInadmissible constructions.
  • Persistence provider boundaries, two invalid offsets, Cache/WideColumn merge, and all accumulating Ingest/Residence families.

Exact 472-file consumer ledger

This is the structural bijection for every current C# planning file containing a project fault/error member invocation. C# comments are stripped without altering string/character literals. Reconciled totals: member 5,677; construction 4,171; Fin.Fail 3,228; MapFail 413; Error.New 35; Error.Many 24; catch 135; throw 88; ToError 424; type-test 60; Code 158; Category 68. The newline-joined 472-row body SHA-256 is e43afb67cf1276e1c35b57e1e0a358bc3ec0007b1e3b6d91dd72f921a78d5851. “Mapped” means atomic participation, not an independently landable slice.

09 · Execution campaign

All work edits existing owner surfaces. The rows below are proof dependencies, not independently landable product phases. Tooling is proved against isolated specimens first; then kernel, every family, every consumer, wire schema/data custody, doctrine, and deletion of the old model form one unaccepted atomic rewrite. The first workspace compile occurs only after old-pattern census reaches zero—there is no intermediate build, merge, or review state with two paradigms.

Planning-fence closure now

Read-only extraction, case/file bijection, code decisions, forbidden-shape census, HTML integrity, and doctrine consistency only. Never compile, analyze, test, cover, or mutate Markdown.

Future source realization

After lifecycle authorization, perform the atomic source rewrite, combined-generator integration, build/analyzers, MTP, architecture/wire proofs, coverage, and mutation. No product source exists as a transient compatibility state.

GateExisting owners editedWorkExit condition
G0 · Decision/data custodyKernel rails; Identity, Coordination, Cache, Codec, Immersive, Geometry; proto/manifest/TS ownersApply the authorized code and data-custody tables above to the already-closed semantic-leaf decisions and 472-file ledger; quarantine ambiguous 8350 and old kernel code 0 exactly as specified; reserve the removed proto fields.Every issued old identity and every consumer site follows its recorded final disposition; no implementation-time architecture choice remains.

C# and Thinktecture retain their own partial/sealed/record/generic diagnostics. No analyzer Catalog row/new docs file is created.

Central files prove universal mechanics only; package-specific behavior is not falsely claimed under the no-new-file constraint.

Per-family migration receipt

old owner · old family · old case/row · old band/offset/code · payload · producer paths · consumer paths · boundary exposure · final class · final owner/family/case · final offset/code · recovery · cause policy · persistence/wire status

  1. Record every column above while applying the current-family ledger and its explicit exception tables; this receipt captures the edit, not a new design decision.
  2. Use the pinned disposition for each old case: retained local fault, universal kernel fault, ordinary absence, success evidence/receipt, programmer invariant, exceptional foreign error, proven unminted reservation deletion, or carrier/wrapper deletion after its information moves to the canonical rail.
  3. Place one explicit offset on every retained local case; preserve published values unless the G0 migration table authorizes a break.
  4. Replace category policy columns with authored case behavior or genuine payload vocabularies; replace aliases by canonical case renames.
  5. Convert every producer and consumer in the same slice; then remove the old roster/root/factory/aggregate surface.
  6. Recount authored nonblank LOC, types, cases, factories, switches, and generated metadata. A case-count reduction is accepted only for carrier Aggregate/wrapper cases or a documented reclassification that preserves the original information.

11 · Proof and acceptance gates

Current-corpus read-only gates

Until design fences become source, verification is structural. The campaign must not compile, test, or run analyzers against .planning Markdown.

Forbidden shapeTargetClosed allowlist
: Expected, constructor-coded Expected, RegistryFault, IFaultRegistry, FaultRegistry, fault *Category0None.
Family Semigroup<*Fault>, Aggregate carrier case, six-line Combine fold0Semantic domain types whose name happens to be Aggregate are classified separately by base/ownership.
Validation<PackageFault,...>, IValidationError<PackageFault>, CreateValidationError, package Create(string)0Thinktecture default ValidationError factory surface only.
Handwritten fault Code/Category/offset switches, absolute Gh/Rhino codes, drift guards, FaultCluster/Seat/OfMessage/GeometryError0Generated identity and central FaultBand rows only.
throw new *Fault/*Error; new InvalidOperationException(fault.Message)0Host ABI egress calls Error.ToException() after custody; programmer/static invariant exceptions remain explicitly classified.
Error.New expected string remints; message/code/category parsing; failed:<code>0Foreign exceptional capture retains original Error; no string identity grammar.
FaultDetail.package, .case, string evidence map, handwritten TS case union, dual decoder0Reserved protobuf coordinates are declarations, not live fields.
FabricationFault.ToError()0None; Geometry's old ToError also deletes.
Polly outside outbound HTTP0One HTTP policy owner only.
FaultBand span widening; a family grown into a neighbour's free tail; restrided successors0A second owner-qualified band row is the one growth path; one band carries one semantic family.
Try.lift(...).Run() or IO.lift capturing a throwing body0Narrow tokenless synchronous lifts whose body raises no cancellation, timeout, or aggregate.

Run these predicates over extracted C# fences—not prose—during planning. The structural estate audit additionally proves every [FaultCase] belongs to a sealed leaf in a closed branch, every issued offset is unique and 0 ≤ offset < Span, every fault interval is disjoint, one band has one family, every code resolves one owner, and every old leaf/file appears in its receipt. Any nonzero match without a named path and rationale fails closure.

Generator diagnostics

  • Missing/duplicate/negative FaultCase; metadata on a noncase; unaccounted direct case; a Fault-derived root missing [Union]. C# owns attribute-constant validity and Thinktecture owns its shipped union-shape diagnostics.
  • Root does not derive Fault, lacks the exact root-owned FamilyBand binding, or a case is not a direct sealed record leaf. Abstract intermediate fault records are forbidden because Thinktecture 10.4 record unions do not support them.
  • Handwritten Identity/IdentityCore/Code competes with generated ownership, or a positional payload actually collides with a generated/base member. Rename the three provider payloads named Code to ProviderCode. Ordinary domain vocabulary is left to the compiler and owner review; the generator is not a naming linter.
  • A second root owns the same band, even with disjoint offsets; fully qualified hint-name collision proof repairs both the new fault path and the existing SelfOp path.
  • Generator diagnostics receive release-ledger rows, positive/negative facts, precise locations, and incremental-cache tests in existing analyzer surfaces.

Runtime and behavioral laws

LawRequired witness
Fault identitySame leaf with different payload returns the same cached FaultId reference; different leaves have different IDs; var copy = id with { }; creates a distinct instance with the same Code that compares and hashes alike.
Fault value equalitySame case + same payload equals and hashes alike; different case or payload differs; collection payloads use structural equality only where consumed.
Carrier preservationFin.Map/Bind preserve exact Error; continuation is not invoked on failure; Validation<Error> accumulates all independent typed leaves and flattens once.
Exception captureUnknown exception retains type/stack/inner; cancellation remains cancellation; documented provider mapping occurs once; null/false return refusals are expected values.
RecoveryTruth table for single and Many errors: terminal dominance, greatest throttle delay, transient bound, cancellation, success after failure, and exhaustion under FakeTimeProvider.
WireCode/recovery/common envelope survives gRPC detail round trip; removed fields/names are reserved; unknown status-detail types coexist; a recognized malformed FaultDetail becomes a typed decode refusal; unknown codes remain opaque RemoteFault; the two behavior-bearing slots retain the admitted envelope structurally and no RPC-owned fault-evidence oneof exists; Mapperly never discriminates the union.
LoggingFakeLogger record state asserts fields independently of rendered text; an actual exceptional Inner is projected as cause evidence, while expected faults never synthesize exceptions.
AllocationBuilt-estate discovery proves all bands, roots, cases, offsets, and code uniqueness; the two historical invalid rows are regression witnesses.

Use only existing files: extend Spec.cs, Gens.cs, Rasm.Architecture.Tests.csproj, TestInfrastructurePrimitives.spec.cs, and the existing generator spec/snapshot. In the architecture csproj, glob exactly the 11 current libs/csharp/*/*.csproj entries into FaultProofProject, feed those items to ProjectReference, and emit one assembly-metadata value from each filename. The spec reads that exact metadata list, loads every named assembly, and fails on a missing assembly; AppDomain.GetAssemblies() and copy-local globbing are insufficient. Replace the old Spec.FaultBands(Owner,Start,End,Codes) gauge with Band · PackageOwner · Family · Kind · Start · Span · Offset · Code. It asserts band↔family bijection, per-kind interval disjointness, attribute offset = code − start, code = Band.Code(offset), global code uniqueness, and nonempty assembly/band/family/leaf sets. Multiple bands per package are valid. Under the hard no-new-file constraint these central files prove universal identity, carrier, recovery, allocation, and wire mechanics—not package-specific message/payload/provider behavior.

12 · Rejected designs

Rejected approachWhy it fails the branch
Keep XCategory and merely generate constructorsPreserves two discriminants, singleton row allocation, runtime registration, and the root source of 1,576 authored LOC.
Generate another category enum or mega fault catalogMoves the mirror into generated output and centralizes downstream vocabulary in kernel; package ownership and standalone use degrade.
Derive code from declaration orderInsertion/reordering silently restrides published identity. Append-only stability requires one explicit integer.
Hash type/case namesSmall band spans force collisions and salt/version policy; renames become accidental wire migrations.
Use runtime reflection or Thinktecture.Internal.MetadataCold-start registration, trimming/AOT risk, unstable internal API, and failure after deployment instead of at compilation.
Duplicate band base/span in attributes or a manifestCreates a second allocation authority. A stale manifest can “prove” the wrong interval.
Static-abstract family interfaceNo genuine generic consumer needs it; runtime-unknown errors still require switching/reflection. An instance-polymorphic base is denser.
Derive from LanguageExt ExpectedIts constructor backing fields participate in equality, deconstruction, and copying; placeholder state plus generated virtual overrides is semantically inconsistent.
Generic Fault(descriptor, object payload) or metadata dictionaryErases exhaustive typed evidence, pushes casts to consumers, and replaces union spam with string-key/dictionary fragility.
Family-local Aggregate/Semigroup plus Error.ManyTwo accumulation paradigms disagree on flattening and recovery; several current Validation<TFault> uses are already algebraically invalid.
Map every exception to a generic expected faultDestroys type, stack, cause, and exceptionality. Only documented provider refusals earn typed expected mapping.
Use Mapperly/QuikGraph/Polly/HighPerformance as the fault modelEach owns a different layer—projection, graphs, root resilience, hot memory. None supplies closed typed failure identity and ROP laws.
Introduce Mediator, OneOf, or another Result packageAdds dispatch/carrier planes with no missing capability and creates the dual paradigm explicitly forbidden by the campaign.
Compatibility aliases or dual wire fieldsLeave two truths and indefinite cleanup. Deliberate numeric migrations are versioned at the owning boundary and the old runtime path is removed.
Claim LOC by deleting cases or call sitesConflates semantic loss with density. Only actual authored diff after preserving each observation counts.
Close condition: an internal-only fault grows by one typed case plus one explicit ordinal. No roster, constructor plumbing, category/case string, aggregate algebra, runtime reflection, wire switch, or kernel catalog edit is required unless the family genuinely needs a new global band. If the new payload crosses a typed boundary, exhaustive union dispatch must fail compilation until that boundary projection is authored.
